# Quit Bad Habits & Addiction (App Store ID 1658588991) — Full Review Analysis

**Corpus:** all **614** text reviews in `app store review/90. Quit Bad Habits & Addiction - Sobriety Counter & Tracker/reviews.jsonl`
**Window:** 2023-01-31 → 2026-09-06 (1,315 days; 431 of the 614 reviews fall on or after 2025-01-01) · **Storefronts:** 37 · **Languages:** 14 · **Corpus mean rating: 4.397★**
**Developer:** SHPAVDA, TOO (bundle `com.shpavda.bad-habit-break`; in-app name "Bad Habit Break") · **Released:** 2023-01-17 · **Current version:** 1.2.2 (2026-07-30) · **Store name in Russia:** Без вредных привычек: дни
**Method:** every review read individually in its original language; 79 theme codes across 12 families (76 hand-assigned, 3 mechanical) and 22 composite unions (plus the derived `U:negative_any`); 101 integrity checks; 26 absence tests; external evidence captured 13 September 2026.

---

## How to read this

Every quantified claim carries a **count, a percentage, the denominator, the market scope, the time window, a signal label and exact review IDs**. Review IDs are `review_id` values from `reviews.jsonl`; the bracketed `#n` is the working index (date order) used by Part 10, so any number resolves to specific reviews and any review resolves to the findings it supports. §9.J lists every review ID behind every code.

Theme counts are **non-exclusive** — a five-star review that praises the counter and asks for a widget is counted in both. Composite unions (`U:` prefix) count each review **once**. Unless a sentence names a narrower scope, the denominator is **614 reviews, all 37 storefronts, 2023-01-31 → 2026-09-06**. Claims written as `` `CODE` = n (x%) `` always use that denominator; segment rates are written "n of N".

### ⚠️ Nine warnings before any number here is used

1. **These reviewers are less positive than the public rating, and one week explains most of the gap.** The same 37 storefronts carry **14,806 public ratings** averaging **4.913★**; this corpus averages **4.397★**, a gap of **+0.515★**. Before the July 2026 crash week the 515 reviews average **4.647★** (§6.7).
2. **Only two storefronts reach the 50-review threshold:** Russia (RU 407) and the United States (US 56) — together 463 of 614 (75.41%). The other 35 storefronts hold 151 reviews, and 17 storefronts have exactly one review. Nothing said about them is more than limited evidence (§6.8).
3. **This is a Russian-language corpus.** 516 reviews (84.04%) are in Russian, including **41 of the 56 US-storefront reviews**. The "US" findings describe mostly Russian speakers using US App Store accounts, not an American audience (§6.4, §6.6). All 14 languages were read in the original; translations are the analyst's own and are labelled.
4. **Reviews carry no version field**, and every release note is one line. Links between a release and what reviewers say are **timing, not proven cause** — though 21 reviewers themselves say the crash began "after the update" (§7.3).
5. **One week holds 69 reviews (11.24%) and 40 of the 62 one- and two-star reviews.** Era E4 (2026-07-16 → 2026-07-22) is a crash wave; §9.D re-runs every headline without it.
6. **281 reviews (45.77%) have under 60 characters of body text.** Helpful votes are rare — 55 reviews (8.96%) — and concentrated: the three most-voted reviews (109, 33 and 32 votes) all ask for tree growth or a widget.
7. **Purchase evidence is almost absent.** `PAID_EXPLICIT` = 2 (0.33%) say they paid; both bought ad removal. That is not a conversion rate, and Part 5 is correspondingly short.
8. **This is a recovery tool for some reviewers, including for self-harm.** `U:safety_sensitive` = 23 (3.75%) name self-harm, drug use or a clinical context (§0.5, §3.11). They are safety-relevant evidence, not only praise.
9. **No review is edited, no text is duplicated, no author repeats.** Short identical titles ("Отличное приложение" 14 times) are common phrasing, not copied reviews; no incentive or referral language appears (§9.H).

---

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

## 0.1 People value a simple, free counter that makes breaking a streak feel costly

**`U:praise_any` = 476 (77.52%, CI 74.1–80.6%, mean 4.81★, High-priority signal)**; **`U:praise_specific` = 285 (46.42%)** name a quality or outcome.

| Code / union | n | % of 614 | What it records |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `PRAISE_SIMPLE_EASY` | 132 | 21.50% | simple, "nothing extra", convenient, intuitive |
| `U:outcome_any` | 118 | 19.22% | the app helped them quit, abstain, or stay motivated |
| `U:tracking_core_praise` | 72 | 11.73% | the counter, several habits at once, or the relapse notes |
| `U:free_or_ads_praise` | 43 | 7.00% | free, no subscription, no or few ads |
| `PRAISE_DESIGN` | 39 | 6.35% | minimal, beautiful, non-triggering design |
| `PRAISE_GENERIC` | 184 | 29.97% | approval with no named quality |

**The mechanism is the number itself.** `10508150052` (#18, 2023-10-23, ru, 4★) — "Рушить большие цифры корежит куда больше, чем желание закурить." *[breaking big numbers hurts far more than the urge to smoke]*. `13053806270` (#289, 2025-08-24, by, 5★), a month without cigarettes — "одна из причин не срываться была то, что все дни обнулятся" *[one reason not to relapse was that all the days would reset]*. `13322337199` (#334, 2025-10-27, ru, 5★) — "Сам факт, что ты видишь четко цифры своих достижений как то больше успокаивают душу, нежели куча похвал." *[seeing the numbers clearly calms me more than a pile of praise]*.

**Minimalism is a feature for this audience.** `12476493945` (#228, 2025-03-29, ru, 5★) — "не триггерит интерфейс (а для людей с зависимостями это важно, лишняя деталь может вывести из себя)" *[the interface doesn't trigger — important for people with addictions, where an extra detail can set you off]*. `13110607514` (#296, 2025-09-07, ru, 5★) lists counter, reset and a diary-like comment "для анализа срыва. Другого и не нужно." *[to analyse a slip. Nothing else is needed]*.

**Free, with many habits at once, is what switchers name.** `10584087680` (#22, 2023-11-14, de, 5★) — "Das ist bisher die einzige kostenfreie App, die dies ohne Abo-Modell möglich macht" *[the only free app so far that allows this without a subscription]*. `12157232469` (#187, 2025-01-07, us, 5★) — "not forcing ads or payments like every other app, only free one out there".

## 0.2 In July 2026 one update made the app unusable for a week

- **`BUG_CRASH` = 65 (10.59%, CI 8.4–13.3%, mean 2.23★, High-priority signal).** 64 of the 65 fall in the seven days 2026-07-16 → 2026-07-22; the only other crash report is `11735356386` (#131, 2024-09-17).
- **The week:** 69 reviews were posted, 64 of them crash reports, at a mean of **2.41★**; it produced 40 of the corpus's 62 one- and two-star reviews. The day after release 1.2.0 (2026-07-15, "The app has been translated into more languages.") the first report arrived: `14309392133` (#516, 2026-07-16, us, 2★) — "После последнего обновления приложение стало вылетать как только заходишь в него" *[after the last update the app crashes as soon as you open it]*. `BUG_AFTER_UPDATE` = 21 (3.42%).
- **Reviewers tie it to the new ad banner.** `BUG_CRASH_AD_LOAD` = 14 (2.28%). `14317429024` (#537, 2026-07-18, ru, 1★) — "After recent update the app constantly crashes after the advertisement screen tries to load!" `14321270649` (#544, 2026-07-19, ru, 3★) — "реклама пытается подгрузиться как только я включаю приложение, и сразу же вылетает" *[the ad tries to load as soon as I open the app, and it crashes at once]*. Offline it works: `14316886107` (#532, us, 1★) — "ПОМОГАЕТ РЕЖИМ ПОЛЕТА" *[airplane mode helps]*; `14317948166` (#541, ru, 3★) — "Если интернет выключен - работает стабильно" *[with the internet off it runs stably]*.
- **The cost was records and users.** `14313036902` (#520, ru, 1★) — "у меня там было много статистики информации и заметок. видимо все потеряла" *[I had lots of statistics and notes there; apparently I've lost it all]*. `14331606843` (#580, us, 3★) — "если случиться «рецидив» нельзя будет тогда в приложении указать" *[if a relapse happens I won't be able to log it]*. `14332778196` (#582, ru, 3★) — "пришлось перейти к конкуренту" *[had to switch to a competitor]*.
- **It was fixed quickly.** Release 1.2.1 shipped 2026-07-21 ("Improved application performance") [external]; on 2026-07-22 `14334098154` (#583, au, 5★) posted "баг с вылетом починили" *[the crash bug was fixed]*. The 30 reviews after 2026-07-22 contain **no** crash report and average 4.70★. The same crash also hit the developer's habit app: `14317387419` (#535) — "То же самое происходит и с приложением по полезным привычкам от этого же разработчика" *[the same happens with this developer's good-habits app]*.

## 0.3 A widget is the single most requested thing, in every year

- **`REQ_WIDGET` = 86 (14.01%, CI 11.5–17.0%, mean 4.53★, High-priority signal)** — first on 2023-02-06 (`9590848342`, #2), last on 2026-08-25 (`14469862599`, #607); 19.3% of E1 reviews and still 14.4% of E3.
- **It decides the fourth or fifth star.** 30 of the 87 four-star reviews (34%) ask for one. `12636973228` (#251, 2025-05-09, ru, 4★) — "но у приложения нет виджета" *[but the app has no widget]*. `13612784119` (#387, 2026-01-09, ru, 3★) — "без виджета нет смысла в приложении" *[without a widget the app is pointless]*. `14103004927` (#476, 2026-05-25, kz, 4★) — "Нет виджетов на домашний экран … удаляю" *[no home-screen widgets … deleting]*.
- **The reason is visibility, which is the product's whole mechanism.** `12248437252` (#203, ru, 4★) — "без него можно и забыть, что это приложение вообще установлено" *[without one you can forget the app is even installed]*. `10929591148` (#48, 32 helpful votes) — "не хватает виджета со счётчиком на экран блокировки" *[missing a lock-screen widget with the counter]*.
- **One paying intent is conditional on it.** `10456809560` (#14, 2023-10-09, ru, 5★) — "Готов купить платную подписку, но прошу добавьте виджеты" *[ready to buy a subscription, but please add widgets]*. `11602446340` (#112) says "на айпаде виджет есть" *[the iPad has a widget]* — a claim this analysis could not check.

## 0.4 Users want the counter to show progress, not only count it

- **`U:motivation_layer_request` = 49 (7.98%, mean 4.35★, High-priority signal)** — tree growth, milestone notifications, quotes and achievements, colours.
- **Tree growth:** `REQ_TREE_GROWTH` = 28 (4.56%, CI 3.2–6.5%, Very strong signal), **27 of them from the RU storefront**. The corpus's most-voted review (109 votes) is `10801415396` (#35, 2024-01-09, ru, 5★) — "почему бы не добавить «рост» дерева?" *[why not add tree growth?]*. `10450985364` (#13, 12 votes) — "сейчас дерево не меняется, закрывается только круг потихоньку" … "И это не особо мотивирует меня" *[the tree doesn't change, only the circle slowly closes … it doesn't motivate me much]*. By 2025 reviewers are impatient: `12966085325` (#279, 2025-08-02, ru, 2★) — "за столько лет у разрабов так и не хватило скилла, чтобы добавить рост дерева" *[in all these years the developers haven't managed to add tree growth]*.
- **Goals:** `U:goal_setting` = 25 (4.07%, Very strong signal) — `GOAL_STEPS_RIGID` = 15 (2.44%) and `GOAL_HOWTO_QUESTION` = 13 (2.12%). `10814511549` (#37, ru, 4★) — "после 7 дней сразу 30" *[after 7 days straight to 30]*; `13804456581` (#435, ru, 4★), sober 13 years, against a 100-year goal — "Если я не пью 13 лет, то логичнее хотеть дотянуть до 15 лет" *[if I've been sober 13 years it makes more sense to aim for 15]*. `13034811450` (#285, 2025-08-19, gb, 3★) says custom goals used to work — "I can't set custom goal times any more just is always 1 day".
- **Notifications:** `REQ_NOTIFICATIONS` = 11 (1.79%). `13305706545` (#330, 2025-10-24, ru, 2★) — "Нет ни ежедневной мотивации, ни уведомлений, ни поздравлений." … "через три дня я забыла, что оно есть" *[no daily motivation, notifications or congratulations … after three days I forgot it existed]*.

## 0.5 For a meaningful minority this is a recovery tool for self-harm and drugs

- **`U:safety_sensitive` = 23 (3.75%, CI 2.5–5.6%, mean 4.96★, Very strong signal)**: `USE_SELF_HARM` = 13 (2.12%), `USE_DRUGS` = 8 (1.30%), `USE_TREATMENT_CONTEXT` = 3 (0.49%).
- `13609431141` (#386, 2026-01-08, ru, 5★) — "с этим приложением я уже месяц не причиняю себе вреда" *[with this app I haven't harmed myself for a month]*. `14176251516` (#494, 2026-06-13, kz, 5★), self-harming for a year — "Это приложение реально помогает сдержаться." *[this app really helps me hold back]*. `13807541941` (#437, us, 5★) — "вот уже почти неделю держалась и следила по приложение, но не сдержалась" *[held on almost a week, tracking in the app, but couldn't]*.
- Drugs and treatment: `12210270920` (#194, by, 5★, 7 votes) — "перестал нюхать кокаин" *[stopped snorting cocaine]*; `13783188576` (#427, us, 5★) — "была в двух рехабах-не помогло" *[two rehabs didn't help]*; `14057707333` (#473, by, 5★) keeps the relapse log for a psychiatrist — "Психиатру так удобнее" *[it's easier for my psychiatrist]*.
- The listing describes quitting smoking, alcohol, gambling and "nail-biting"; it does not mention self-harm [external]. **What follows:** the app is already used for self-harm recovery by reviewers who are likely young; its content, relapse copy and any ads shown to them are a safety question (§8.7).

## 0.6 The free, ad-light app that reviewers praised is now criticised for its ads

- **Before June 2024 no review complains about ads**, and `PRAISE_NO_FEW_ADS` = 19 (3.09%) starts on day one: `9568198570` (#1, 2023-01-31) — "классно , что без рекламы" *[great that there are no ads]*.
- **`U:ads_negative` = 20 (3.26%, mean 2.65★, Very strong signal)** from 2024-06-12 to 2026-06-20. `11374484206` (#90, co, 2★) — "15 segundos obligatorios de publicidad cada que uno usa la aplicación" *[15 mandatory seconds of ads every time you use the app]*. `13116009951` (#297, 2025-09-09, ru, 1★) — ads every two minutes, and "что бы ее закрыть , нужно выйти полностью" *[to close it you have to exit completely]*. 6 of the 20 say they deleted the app.
- **Gambling ads inside an addiction app.** `ADS_HARMFUL_CONTENT` = 2 (0.33%, Weak signal — promoted on safety grounds). `12635810456` (#250, 2025-05-09, ru, 3★) — "рекламирует им ставки и казино" … "Может сразу уже рекламу алкоголя и сигарет вставите?" *[it advertises betting and casinos to them … why not add alcohol and cigarette ads too?]*. `13174041563` (#307, 2025-09-23, ru, 1★, titled "Развод" *[scam]*) — "реклама лудомании, одной из зависимостей" *[ads for gambling, one of the addictions]*.
- **In July 2026 the ad banner is what reviewers blame for the crash** (§0.2).

## 0.7 Monetisation barely registers

- The only purchase is ad removal: Monthly Ad-Free **$0.99** / Yearly Ad-Free **$9.99** (RU **99,00 ₽** / **699,00 ₽**) [external]. `PAID_EXPLICIT` = 2 (0.33%), both for ad removal and both satisfied: `11651806786` (#122, ru) — "Сразу оплатил, такой трекер нельзя начинать использовать с просмотра вонючей рекламы" *[paid at once — you can't start a tracker like this by watching smelly ads]*; `13254705653` (#318, us) — "Well the were ads before I paid for them."
- **Six Russian reviewers believe the app is paid** — `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` = 6 (0.98%, Emerging signal), all RU storefront. `11890941612` (#153, 1★) — "Даже нельзя посмотреть интерфейс! Полностью платное приложение" *[you can't even see the interface; a fully paid app]*; `13029179785` (#284, 1★) — "сплошная реклама и все только на месяц" *[nothing but ads, and everything only for a month]*.
- **Subscription format, not price, is the objection.** `14162589436` (#490, kz, 3★) — "Грустно, что нельзя пожизненно отключить рекламу." *[sad you can't switch ads off for life]*; `11641762030` (#120, vn, 5★) — "Mình nghĩ nên thêm lựa chọn mua app trọn đời nữa. Mình muốn mua" *[you should add a lifetime purchase; I want to buy]*.

## 0.8 What this corpus shows is *absent*

Tested against all 614 reviews with multilingual patterns; every hit was read (§9.F). **No Android comparison, no privacy or tracking concern — although the listing declares "Data Used to Track You" — no mention of a developer reply or support contact, and no AI request.** Backup or transfer to a new phone is raised 4 times (`REQ_BACKUP_SYNC` = 4, 0.65%). Only one price is quoted by a reviewer (`12839988426`, 100₽).

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `app store review/90. …/reviews.jsonl` | **The corpus.** 614 records, one JSON object per line. |
| `app store review/90. …/by_country/*.jsonl` | 37 per-storefront files, 614 records total. |
| `app store review/90. …/manifest.json` | Extraction metadata (2026-09-08): 614 reviews, 37 storefronts, rating distribution 439/87/26/17/45, mean 4.397. |
| `app store review/90. …/_state.json` | Crawl state for **112 storefronts**: 37 with reviews, 75 swept and empty, 0 incomplete. |

The source folder is read-only; nothing was written to it. All working artefacts are in `temp/` (`90-*`).

## 1.2 Schema actually present

13 fields, populated on all 614 records, with no nulls and no empty strings:

| Field | Notes |
|---|---|
| `review_id` | Primary key. **614 unique, no duplicates.** 11 digits (609) or 10 digits (5). |
| `app_id` / `app_name` | 1658588991 / `Quit Bad Habits & Addiction` throughout. |
| `country` / `country_name` | 37 storefronts. **Storefront, not language** (§6.6). |
| `rating` | 1–5, all five values present. |
| `title` | "Отличное приложение" and "." 14 times each; "Супер" and "Вылетает" 11 each. No title+body pair repeats. |
| `body` | Five short bodies appear twice ("Добавьте виджет", "помогает", "."…); median body length 65 characters. |
| `author` | All 614 distinct. **No repeat authors.** |
| `date` | ISO-8601 UTC. Up to 14 reviews on one calendar day (§9.H). |
| `vote_count` / `vote_sum` | 55 reviews carry helpful votes; maximum 109; 277 votes in total. |
| `is_edited` | **false on all 614.** |

**There is no `version` field.**

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

Run by `90-reconcile.py`; output in `temp/90-reconcile-out.txt`. Every check is clean.

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Records read from `reviews.jsonl` | **614 — 100% of the corpus** |
| Unique `review_id` | 614 (**0 duplicates**) |
| Records across the 37 `by_country` files | 614; ID set identical to the main file |
| Country records not identical, field by field, to the main-file record | **0** |
| Country field ≠ file name | **0** |
| `manifest.json` total / per-country counts / storefront count / rating distribution | **all match exactly** |
| Mean rating recomputed vs manifest | 4.397 vs 4.397 ✓ |
| `_state.json` collected counts vs main file | **0 differences**; 112 storefronts swept, 0 incomplete |
| Exact title+body duplicates | **0** (five short bodies repeat with different titles) |
| Repeat author names | **0** |

**No deduplication was necessary or performed.**

## 1.4 Processing method

1. `90-dump.py` — parse all 614 records, HTML-unescape, sort by `(date, review_id)`, emit `90-all-reviews.txt` (every review in full) and `90-index.tsv`.
2. **Every review read individually, in full, in its original language, in date order.** No sampling, no keyword filtering, no clustering. Russian (516), English (42), Ukrainian (15), Spanish (12), German (5), Turkish and Portuguese (4 each), Polish and Vietnamese (3 each), Japanese (2), Norwegian, Italian, French and Arabic (1 each), plus two emoji-only reviews and two with letters but no language.
3. `90-themes.py` — a 79-code, 12-family taxonomy built **from** the corpus after reading it, with a definition and direction for every code, plus 22 unions and the derived `U:negative_any`. **Every code is used.**
4. `90-cls/001-100.txt` … `501-614.txt` — the hand classification, six files: one line per review with the working index, the `review_id`, its codes, its language and a note quoting the exact words that drove each code (with translations). **This is the expensive artefact.**
5. `90-build-classification.py` — asserts every line's `review_id` against the index rebuilt from `reviews.jsonl`, refuses unknown codes, duplicate lines, unclassified reviews or hand-assigned mechanical codes, then emits `90-review-classification.py` (CLASSIFICATION, THEME_TO_IDS, LANG, META, NOTES). `META_LOW_INFO`, `META_HAS_VOTES` and `META_NON_RUSSIAN` are assigned **mechanically**; there is no `META_EDITED` because no review is edited.
6. `90-validate.py` — **101 checks, all passing**: 10 structural (zero unknown IDs, zero intra-theme duplicates, zero unassigned records, every code used), 3 mechanical checks plus one asserting no review is edited, 1 language, 57 forward text-to-code ties, 18 reverse vocabulary tests with every hit printed, 11 consistency rules. **The first run failed 18 checks**; 2 were real classification errors (§9.C).
7. `90-aggregate.py` — every count, union, star band, storefront, eligible-country table, market group, language, era, month, monetisation segment, sensitivity run, vote and burst check, absence test and price point; writes `90-gen/`.
8. `90-build-report.py` assembles this report from hand-written sources and generated tables; `90-verify-report.py` re-derives its claims afterwards (§9.M).

## 1.5 Signal thresholds

Applied to the global corpus (denominator 614) and separately to RU (407) and US (56) with their own denominators.

| Share | Label |
|---|---|
| <0.1% | Ignore by default |
| 0.1–<0.5% | Weak signal |
| 0.5–<1% | Emerging signal |
| 1–<3% | Meaningful signal |
| 3–5% | Very strong signal |
| >5% | High-priority signal |

**A caution on this scale at n = 614.** One review is 0.16% (Weak), four are 0.65% (Emerging), seven are 1.14% (Meaningful) and 31 are 5.05% (High-priority). In the US a single review is 1.8%. Read the count and the confidence interval, not only the label, for anything under ten. Two findings are promoted below their label on safety grounds: gambling ads (`ADS_HARMFUL_CONTENT`, 2) and data loss (`DATA_LOSS`, 3).

## 1.6 Limitations, stated plainly

1. **One crash week** (warning 5) dominates the negative evidence; §9.D shows what remains without it.
2. **Two eligible storefronts**, and the US one is mostly Russian-language.
3. **Short reviews.** 45.77% are under 60 characters; many say only "помогает" *[helps]* or "Супер".
4. **No version field; one-line release notes.** Eras are defined from the corpus and the release dates.
5. **Purchases, outcomes and substance use are self-reported**, and some may be jokes (`META_OFF_TOPIC_JOKE` = 5, 0.81%; §9.C lists the ambiguous drug mentions).
6. **The app was not installed or tested**; ads, paywall screens and the goal ladder could not be observed.
7. **Translations are the analyst's own.**
8. **Non-exclusive counts.** Codes overlap by design; unions give once-per-review counts.

---

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 What the product is, from the reviews

A **sobriety / abstinence counter**. The user adds any number of bad habits; each shows time since quitting to the second, a **current goal** that steps up automatically (reviewers cite 1, 7 and 30 days, a month, and 100 years), a **personal record**, an **attempts counter**, and a **relapse button** that resets the timer and can store a **note with a reason and date**. The home screen shows a **tree logo inside a filling circle**. There is an **articles** section, a colour per habit, a **dark theme**, and **ads** with a paid ad-free option. There are **no notifications, no widget, no account and no goal editing** according to reviewers.

| Feature | Evidence in reviews | Representative IDs |
|---|---|---|
| Counter, records, goals | `PRAISE_COUNTER_PROGRESS` 48; `GOAL_STEPS_RIGID` 15; `GOAL_HOWTO_QUESTION` 13 | `10508150052`, `13053806270`, `13796263239`, `10814511549` |
| Several habits at once | `PRAISE_MULTI_HABITS` 17 | `9568198570`, `10584087680`, `12236129854` |
| Relapse log and notes | `PRAISE_NOTES_LOG` 21; `REQ_NOTES_EDITING` 7; `REQ_RELAPSE_OPTIONS` 4 | `11090976286`, `13110607514`, `13755233141` |
| Tree / circle visual | `REQ_TREE_GROWTH` 28; tree logo praised (`14274384633`) | `10801415396`, `10450985364` |

| Capability | Status | Evidence |

| Removing ads | **Paid** — monthly or yearly subscription | listing; `PAID_FOR_AD_REMOVAL` 2 |

| Goal duration / custom goals | **Unclear** — one reviewer asks if it is paid-only (`10306903519`); others say it cannot be set | `U:goal_setting` 25 |
| Trial | **Unclear** — one reviewer describes a "free trial for a month" that still tried to charge after cancelling (`13524890756`); the listing shows no trial | `TRIAL_USER` 1 |
| Perceived as paid | **Belief, not listing** — six RU reviewers call it paid or "only for a month" | `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` 6 |

## 2.4 Data and privacy statements

| Source | What it says |
|---|---|
| App Store privacy label | **Data Used to Track You** (location, identifiers, usage data, diagnostics) and **Data Not Linked to You** for third-party advertising and analytics |
| Description | "Works offline"; privacy policy and terms linked at shpavda.com |

**No reviewer raises privacy, tracking or data use** (§9.F). Reviewers do name what they track — self-harm, cocaine, injected drugs, masturbation (§0.5, §3.4) — in an app whose label declares tracking for advertising. Four reviewers ask how to keep their data on a new phone, and one fears losing "много статистики информации и заметок" *[lots of statistics and notes]* in the crash (`14313036902`).

## 2.5 What could not be established

- **Downloads, conversion, revenue, refund and churn rates.**
- **When ads were introduced or changed.** `9742639169` (#3, 2023-03-23) already says "kun reklame" *[only ads]*; complaints start 2024-06-12; two crash reviewers describe ads as newly added in July 2026 (`14314195428`, `14321270649`).
- **Whether the paywall screens reviewers describe exist**, or are ad-removal offers read as a paywall.
- **What the goal ladder is** and whether custom goals were removed (`13034811450` says they were).
- **Which ad networks and categories are served**, including the gambling ads two reviewers report.
- **What caused the July 2026 crash**, beyond what reviewers report (ad banner, network on, after 1.2.0).


---

# PART 3 — GLOBAL FINDINGS

All denominators are 614 reviews, all 37 storefronts, 2023-01-31 → 2026-09-06, unless stated. Counts are non-exclusive; unions count each review once.

Six findings carry the corpus:

1. **Delight in simplicity** — `U:praise_any` = 476 (77.52%): a minimal, free counter that works (§3.2).
2. **Outcomes, including recovery** — `U:outcome_any` = 118 (19.22%); `U:safety_sensitive` = 23 (3.75%) (§3.3, §3.4, §3.11).
3. **Requests from satisfied users** — `U:request_any` = 164 (26.71%, mean 4.45★), led by the widget (§3.8).
4. **Reliability** — `U:crash_any` = 65 (10.59%), all but one in one week (§3.5, §7.3).
5. **Ads** — `U:ads_negative` = 20 (3.26%), including gambling ads (§3.6).
6. **Goals** — `U:goal_setting` = 25 (4.07%): a fixed goal ladder users cannot understand or change (§3.7).

`U:negative_any` = 119 (19.38%) contain at least one negative code, at a mean of 2.59★.

- **Simple and convenient.** `PRAISE_SIMPLE_EASY` = 132 (21.50%, mean 4.91★). `11385749806` (#92, 2024-06-15, us, 5★) — "никаких подписок и лишних кнопок" *[no subscriptions and no extra buttons]*.
- **The number is the motivation.** `PRAISE_COUNTER_PROGRESS` = 48 (7.82%, mean 4.94★). `12237576860` (#200, 2025-01-27, ru, 5★) dreams of smoking and is "в ужасе, что придется в приложении отмечать «попытку N2»" *[horrified at having to log attempt 2 in the app]*.
- **Design that stays out of the way.** `PRAISE_DESIGN` = 39 (6.35%). `14274384633` (#513, us) — "Идея с деревом как главной иконки и просто красивая" *[the idea of the tree as the main icon is simply beautiful]*.
- **Free and several habits at once.** `PRAISE_FREE` = 30 (4.89%); `PRAISE_MULTI_HABITS` = 17 (2.77%); `PRAISE_NO_FEW_ADS` = 19 (3.09%). `12265272270` (#205, ua, 4★) — "Пока это лучшее бесплатное приложение что я находила." *[so far the best free app I've found]*.
- **The relapse note.** `PRAISE_NOTES_LOG` = 21 (3.42%). `11090976286` (#62, 2024-03-27, jp, 5★), severe phone addiction — "再発してしまった時に理由を書いて保存できる機能が素晴らしいと思いました" *[the ability to write and save the reason when you relapse is wonderful]*. `12305497364` (#212, ua) uses notes to avoid a reset — "Я не хотела из-за одного дня срыва обнулять достижения 2 недель, поэтому просто записала это день." *[I didn't want one day's slip to wipe two weeks, so I just noted the day]*.
- **Best in category.** `PRAISE_BEST` = 20 (3.26%). **Generic praise** `PRAISE_GENERIC` = 184 (29.97%) — 281 reviews overall are under 60 characters.

- **Quit or abstaining.** `OUT_QUIT_ABSTAINING` = 42 (6.84%, CI 5.1–9.1%, mean 4.86★). `13909943404` (#449, 2026-04-01, ua, 5★) quit the day she installed it — "не курю уже 2 год" *[not smoking for 2 years]*. `12061048113` (#178, ru) — "С приложением бросил курить , наркотики" *[quit smoking and drugs with the app]*.

**`U:defect_any` = 70 (11.40%, mean 2.34★); `U:crash_any` = 65 (10.59%).**

- **The crash week** — §0.2 and §7.3. Devices named include iPhone 13, iPhone 14, iPhone 14 Pro Max on iOS 26.5.2 (`14317316998`) and iPhone 17 Pro (`14326924586`); reinstalling did not help (`14325996890`, `14330378148`). `CRASH_OFFLINE_WORKAROUND` = 3 (0.49%).
- **Counter errors.** `BUG_COUNTER_ATTEMPTS` = 5 (0.81%): the count follows the phone clock — `11644642764` (#121) "со сменой даты в телефоне отсчёт тоже меняется" *[changing the phone's date changes the count]*; attempt 2 not recorded (`11876692458`); a deleted relapse still counts (`13755233141`); a wrong day count after the update (`14316874234`).
- **Lost data.** `DATA_LOSS` = 3 (0.49%, Weak — kept on data-loss grounds): `11194993574` (#72) — "А она удалилась сама" *[and it deleted itself]*; `12207978135` (#192) — "неделю не заходи, он скинул к нулю 1000 день" *[don't open it for a week and it reset day 1000 to zero]*; `14313036902` (#520).
- **Thin product.** `FEATURE_THIN` = 12 (1.95%, mean 3.33★). `13964268722` (#460, 1★) — "тупо таймер: старт - стоп" *[just a timer: start, stop]*; `10960263124` (#52, 1★) — "не сложнее таймера" *[no more complex than a timer]*.
- **Maintenance.** `NEGLECT_PERCEIVED` = 3 (0.49%), all during the 19 months without a release: `12867989318` (#268, 2025-07-08) — "Будут ли какие-то обновления? Судя по тому когда было последнее - чувство, что не стоит ждать." *[will there be updates? judging by the last one, I feel I shouldn't wait]*. `REDESIGN_DISLIKE` = 1: `14371521430` (#587, 2026-07-31) — "Старый дизайн был лучше. Зачем добавили кнопку архивировать?" *[the old design was better; why add an archive button?]*.

- **Ads.** `ADS_TOO_MANY` = 18 (2.93%, mean 2.72★). `14205925648` (#498, 2026-06-20, ru, 1★) — "После каждого действия - долгая реклама. Удаляю." *[a long ad after every action; deleting]*. `13521151325` (#373, 1★) — "Реклама длинная и не проматывается." *[the ads are long and can't be skipped]*. Some accept it: `13822626598` (#440, 4★) — "В бесплатной версии чересчур много рекламы, но на это уже бесполезно жаловаться" *[far too many ads in the free version, but it's pointless complaining]*; `14508678503` (#611, 5★) — "реклама в полне оправдана" *[the ads are fully justified]*.
- **Gambling ads.** `ADS_HARMFUL_CONTENT` = 2 — §0.6.

- **Perceived paywall.** `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` = 6 (0.98%) — §0.7. `10306903519` (#10, 3★) — "или это только в платной версии?" *[or is it only in the paid version?]*; `13840823158` (#442, 5★) — "Приложение хорошее,жаль платное" *[the app is good, pity it's paid]*.
- **Subscription objection.** `SUBSCRIPTION_OBJECTION` = 2 (0.33%): `12839988426` (#266, 3★) — "несчастные 100₽) на подписку и я тупо удалил приложение" *[a measly 100₽ on a subscription, and I just deleted the app]*.

## 3.7 Goals

| Code | Dir | n | % of 614 | CI 95% | Mean ★ | Signal | Review IDs |
|---|:-:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| `GOAL_STEPS_RIGID` | ~ | 15 | 2.44% | 1.5–4.0% | 3.87 | Meaningful signal | `10814511549`, `11188197797`, `11570561584`, `11823352857`, `12295412260`, `12689955719`, `12867989318`, `12901990487`, `12975458293`, `13034811450`, +5 more (§9.J) |
| `GOAL_HOWTO_QUESTION` | o | 13 | 2.12% | 1.2–3.6% | 4.38 | Meaningful signal | `10306903519`, `11015941156`, `11238291017`, `11353723830`, `12689955719`, `12774617599`, `12867989318`, `12951886651`, `13183842811`, `13298257521`, +3 more (§9.J) |

**`U:goal_setting` = 25 (4.07%, mean 4.12★, Very strong signal)** — a request from people who otherwise like the app.

- **The ladder confuses new users.** `12689955719` (#255, 2025-05-23, 3★) — "Всё протыкал, не вижу, где можно поменять цель" *[tapped everything, can't see where to change the goal]*; `12867989318` (#268) — "Первый день пыталась понять почему у меня цель один день и где это поменять." *[spent the first day trying to understand why my goal is one day and where to change it]*.
- **The steps don't fit.** `11823352857` (#144, 4★) — 7 then 30 days is too far for people quitting strong habits; `14456135031` (#604, de, 4★) — "Ziel 100 Jahre ist etwas unrealistisch" *[a 100-year goal is somewhat unrealistic]*; `13445493559` (#360) — "При создании цели автоматом ставит месяц, а мне надо год" *[it sets a month automatically, I need a year]*.
- **Some like it automatic.** `13793876521` (#432, 5★) — "Нравится, что автоматически обновляется цель и дает стимул." *[I like that the goal updates automatically and gives a push]*.

- **Widget** — `REQ_WIDGET` = 86 (14.01%) — §0.3. Lock screen (`9590848342`, `10929591148`), home screen (`11317075866`), with the day count and the wheel (`12930342553`), and to mark progress (`13070652861`). `13724399997` (#410) — "Если добавите виджеты то будет лучшее приложение из этой категории" *[add widgets and it will be the best app in the category]*.
- **Tree growth** — `REQ_TREE_GROWTH` = 28 (4.56%) — §0.4. 8 reviews cite other reviews (`META_CITES_OTHER_REVIEWS` = 8, 1.30%): `13716590558` (#406) — "я наверное выскажу просьбу всех кто им пользуется: ДОБАВЬТЕ ПОЖАЛУЙСТА РОСТ ДЕРЕВА" *[I'll voice everyone's request: PLEASE ADD TREE GROWTH]*.
- **Motivation content** — `REQ_MOTIVATION_CONTENT` = 14 (2.28%): achievements (`11591085925`, `11836674599`), quotes (`13511567781`, `14234441517`), and supportive relapse copy — `10995642477` (#56) — "показывал «могли бы быть чистым столько-то дней»" *[show a line saying how many days you could have been clean]*; `14118955087` (#479) — "Было бы круто добавить кнопку «почти сорвался»" *[a button for "почти сорвался" — almost relapsed]*.
- **Notifications** — `REQ_NOTIFICATIONS` = 11 (1.79%). `11481446444` (#100, us) — "Only downside is no notifications"; `11959471388` (#161) — "приложение очень быстро забывается" *[the app is forgotten very quickly]*.
- **Colours, calendar, notes, relapse options, display.** `REQ_COLOURS_THEMES` = 7 (1.14%) — "цвета слишком токсичные" *[the colours are too toxic]* (`10843849305`); `REQ_NOTES_EDITING` = 7 (1.14%); `REQ_COUNTER_DISPLAY` = 5 (0.81%) — `11058579544` "напрягает отсутствие количества дней в текущей попытке" *[annoying that there's no day count for the current attempt]*; `REQ_CALENDAR` = 4 (0.65%) — `14380495771` "отображения на календаре в виде точек когда произошел рецидив" *[relapses as dots on a calendar]*; `REQ_RELAPSE_OPTIONS` = 4 (0.65%) — `12031517995` "нельзя поставить на стоп и оставить прогресс" *[you can't pause and keep your progress]*.
- **Backup, benefits, sort, positive habits.** `REQ_BACKUP_SYNC` = 4 (0.65%) — `12780782479` "Если решил сменить айфон, прогресс сохраниться?" *[if I change iPhone, will my progress be saved?]*; `REQ_BENEFITS_STATS` = 3 (0.49%) — `12774617599` "Плохо что не показывается сколько сигарет или алкоголя не выпито." *[bad that it doesn't show cigarettes or drinks not consumed]*; `REQ_SORT_ORDER` = 4 (0.65%); `REQ_POSITIVE_HABITS` = 3 (0.49%) — `11848596914` "слова : отказ,вредные привычки, рецидив, так себе" *[the words refusal, bad habits, relapse are so-so]*.

- **Questions in reviews.** `SUPPORT_QUESTION` = 15 (2.44%, mean 3.73★), 14 of them RU: how to set the goal, the start date, whether progress transfers, how to fix the crash. **No reviewer mentions a reply** (§9.F, §9.I).
- **Churn.** `U:lost_user_signal` = 13 (2.12%, mean 1.92★): `CHURN_STATED` = 11 (1.79%), `CHURN_RISK` = 2. Stated reasons: ads (`11675365714`, `12346475330`, `13116009951`, `14205925648`, `12839988426`, `13029179785`), the crash (`14326452102`, `14332778196`), no widget (`14103004927`), nothing happening in the app (`13305706545`), a charge after cancelling (`13524890756`).
- **Trust.** `SCAM_LANGUAGE` = 4 (0.65%): `10097280052` "Kandırmaca" *[deception]* over an unexpected charge; `13174041563` "Развод" *[scam]* over gambling ads; `11374484206` "avariciosos" *[greedy]*; and `10856120064` "Отзывы походу купленные." *[the reviews seem bought]*.

## 3.11 Privacy and safety

- **Self-harm.** `USE_SELF_HARM` = 13 (2.12%, CI 1.2–3.6%, mean 4.92★) — 12 of 13 describe stopping or holding back; one describes a lapse (`13807541941`). `13346175054` (#338) — "конечно желание не пропадает, но хотя бы не причиняю себе вреда" *[of course the urge doesn't go, but at least I don't hurt myself]*. `10764145523` (#34, 2023-12-30, ua) — "с ним я перестала плакать,резаться" *[with it I stopped crying and cutting]*.
- **Drugs.** `USE_DRUGS` = 8 (1.30%) — cocaine (`12210270920`), injected drugs (`13946108990`), Lyrica (`13960493073`, who "продержался меньше суток но приложение отлично" *[held out less than a day but the app is great]*), weed (`13744886789`); two may be jokes (§9.C).
- **Relapse copy is emotionally loaded** for this audience: a relapse wipes the counter, and users ask for supportive wording (§3.8).
- **Gambling ads** — §0.6.
- **Privacy.** No reviewer raises privacy, tracking or data (§9.F), against a label that declares data used to track (§2.4).

# PART 4 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

## 4.1 The distribution

| Stars | n | % of 614 | Wilson 95% CI |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| 5★ | 439 | 71.50% | 67.8–74.9% |
| 4★ | 87 | 14.17% | 11.6–17.2% |
| 3★ | 26 | 4.23% | 2.9–6.1% |
| 2★ | 17 | 2.77% | 1.7–4.4% |
| 1★ | 45 | 7.33% | 5.5–9.7% |

**Mean 4.397★, median 5★.** 439 of 614 reviews (71.50%) are five-star and 62 (10.10%) are one- or two-star. The public rating across the same storefronts is 4.913★ (§6.7).

**The bands are about different things.** Five stars is praise (93%) and short text (51%); four stars is praise plus a request — 30 of 87 ask for a widget and 15 for tree growth; three stars and below is the crash (46% of three-star, 71% of two-star, 62% of one-star) and ads. Helpful votes rise as ratings fall (5% of five-star reviews carry votes, 27% of one-star).

## 4.3 Five-star reviews (439, 71.50%)

- **408 of 439 praise the app (93%)**; outcomes appear in 111 (25%), more than in any other band.
- **225 of 439 are under 60 characters** ("Супер", "помогает").
- **52 ask for a widget and 12 for tree growth** while giving five stars.
- **5 are crash reports** and 4 are complaint-only (`META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH`, §4.7): `14314900938` (#524) — "бесконечно вылетает" *[crashes endlessly]*.

## 4.4 Four-star reviews (87, 14.17%)

*Good, but add…* — 53 of 87 praise the app (61%), and **37 of 87 (43%) make a request other than the widget**. The widget (30) and tree growth (15) are the missing star: `12636973228` (#251, 4★) says so outright. `U:goal_setting` peaks here (13 of 87, 15%). 8 are crash reports.

## 4.5 Three-star reviews (26, 4.23%)

**Broken, still liked.** 12 of 26 report a crash (46%) and 10 still praise the app (38%); 5 carry monetisation friction (19%): `12839988426` (ads and a subscription), `14162589436` (no lifetime ad removal), `12635810456` (gambling ads).

## 4.6 Two- and one-star reviews (62, 10.10%)

- **40 of the 62 are crash reports**, and all 40 were posted in the crash week.
- **Ads or paywall:** 12 of the 45 one-star reviews carry monetisation friction (27%); 8 name ads.
- **The 12 that are neither a crash nor ads:** an unexpected charge (`10097280052`, `13524890756`), a perceived full paywall (`10856120064`, `11890941612`), the app dismissed as just a timer (`10960263124` "не сложнее таймера", `13305706545`, `13964268722`), no widget (`11448181173`), no tree growth after years (`12966085325`), a redesign (`14371521430`), and two positive texts at one or two stars (`12305474577`, `14294362470`).

## 4.7 Rating-versus-text contradictions

`META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH` = 16 (2.61%). Retained at the star given:

| # | Review ID | Date | Store | ★ | Codes (non-meta) |
|---:|---|---|---|---:|---|
| 152 | `11876692458` | 2024-10-26 | ru | 4★ | `BUG_COUNTER_ATTEMPTS`, `CHURN_RISK` |
| 211 | `12305474577` | 2025-02-13 | ru | 1★ | `OUT_QUIT_ABSTAINING` |
| 303 | `13152526034` | 2025-09-18 | kr | 5★ | `REQ_OTHER`, `UX_CONFUSING` |
| 355 | `13420117881` | 2025-11-20 | fr | 4★ | `ADS_TOO_MANY` |
| 393 | `13643213864` | 2026-01-17 | us | 4★ | `ADS_TOO_MANY` |
| 440 | `13822626598` | 2026-03-07 | ru | 4★ | `ADS_TOO_MANY`, `REQ_CALENDAR` |
| 476 | `14103004927` | 2026-05-25 | kz | 4★ | `CHURN_STATED`, `REQ_WIDGET` |
| 514 | `14294362470` | 2026-07-12 | us | 2★ | `GOAL_STEPS_RIGID`, `PRAISE_GENERIC` |
| 519 | `14312752399` | 2026-07-17 | ru | 4★ | `BUG_AFTER_UPDATE`, `BUG_CRASH` |
| 524 | `14314900938` | 2026-07-17 | ru | 5★ | `BUG_CRASH` |

**14 of the 16 are four- or five-star reviews with only a complaint** — 7 of them crash reports posted during the crash week, others about ads (`13420117881`, `13643213864`, `13822626598`), the widget (`14103004927`, `14334827225`) or a counter bug (`11876692458`). The other two are praise at one or two stars: `12305474577` (#211, 1★, titled "Помогло бросить" *[helped me quit]*) and `14294362470` (#514, 2★, titled "приложение норм" *[the app's fine]*). **Low stars in this corpus track the crash, not dissatisfaction with the product's concept.**

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 The monetisation model

A **free app with ads**. The only purchases are **ad-removal subscriptions**: Monthly Ad-Free $0.99 and Yearly Ad-Free $9.99 in the US; 99,00 ₽ and 699,00 ₽ in Russia [external]. Subscriptions were added in version 1.1.1 on 2023-04-03. No feature is listed as paid.

| Evidence | Finding |
|---|---|
| Reviewers who say they paid | **`PAID_EXPLICIT` = 2 (0.33%)** — ru 1, us 1; both 5★ |
| …what they paid for | ad removal, 2 of 2 |
| …satisfied | 2 of 2 |
| Reviewers who would pay | `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` = 3 (0.49%) — for a widget (`10456809560`), to support the developer (`11505099084`), for a lifetime option (`11641762030`) |
| Reviewers who describe a trial | `TRIAL_USER` = 1 (0.16%) — `13524890756`, charged after cancelling |
| Any monetisation code | `U:money_any` = 36 (5.86%) — 20 of them ad complaints |

**No conversion, refund or retention rate can be derived from reviews.**

## 5.2 Segment rates

These are **segment rates** (denominators 2, 3, 20, 43 and the remaining reviews), shown beside the global count; they are not threshold classifications. **Reviews that criticise ads are the churn segment**: 7 of the 20 carry a lost-user signal, against 5 of the reviews with no money code.

## 5.3 What buyers say they bought and why

| Trigger | Evidence |
|---|---|
| **Not starting a tracker with ads** | `11651806786` (#122, 2024-08-25, ru, 5★) paid immediately; "после оплаты всплыло шикарное окно, респект вашему маркетологу" *[after paying a gorgeous screen appeared; respect to your marketer]* |
| **Ads were annoying** | `13254705653` (#318, 2025-10-12, us, 5★) — "the were ads before I paid for them. But I like this ap." |
| **Supporting the developer** (intent) | `11505099084` (#103, kz) — "Возможность подписки без рекламы считаю только плюсом, думаю воспользоваться и поддержать." *[I see the ad-free subscription as a plus; thinking of using it to support you]* |
| **A widget** (intent) | `10456809560` (#14) — §0.3 |

## 5.4 What goes wrong after purchase

No paying reviewer reports a problem. The two billing complaints come from reviewers who did not intend to pay: `10097280052` (#7) and `13524890756` (#374) — §3.10.

## 5.5 Upgrade barriers among non-buyers

| Barrier | n | % of 614 | Representative |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Believes the app or a feature is paid (`PAYWALL_PERCEIVED`) | 6 | 0.98% | `11890941612`, `13029179785`, `13840823158` |
| Wants a lifetime purchase (`WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE`) | 2 | 0.33% | `11641762030`, `14162589436` |
| Refuses a subscription (`SUBSCRIPTION_OBJECTION`) | 2 | 0.33% | `12839988426`, `14162589436` |
| Hopes it stays free (`FUTURE_MONETISATION_WORRY`) | 3 | 0.49% | `10683386706`, `13433119107`, `13712536946` |
| Accepts ads instead of paying | — | — | `12548923831` "não é obrigado a pagar, apenas assistir um anúncio" *[you don't have to pay, just watch an ad]*; `13598745387` |

**The barrier is format and framing, not price.** The only amount a reviewer names is 100₽ (`12839988426`), and the one US reviewer to mention price, `14510416958`, calls it "reasonably priced". Ad removal is a subscription; two reviewers ask for it for life.

## 5.6 What cannot be claimed here

- **No conversion rate, and no finding about who pays.** Two paying reviewers are two people.
- **No price-elasticity finding.**
- **No finding that a widget would sell subscriptions.** One reviewer said so in 2023 (`10456809560`); it is an experiment to run, not a result.

## 6.2 Eligibility

The threshold is 50 reviews. **Russia (RU 407) and the United States (US 56) qualify**; together they are 463 of 614 (75.41%). The next largest are Ukraine 33, Kazakhstan 30 and Belarus 13; 17 storefronts have exactly one review. All 614 reviews are in every global figure.

Signal labels use the Russian denominator (407); one review is 0.25%. Russian reviews span 2023–2026 (2023: 24, 2024: 105, 2025: 135, 2026: 143).

**What is distinctive about Russia (with counts):**

- **The requesters.** `REQ_WIDGET` 68 of 407 (16.7%) vs 8.7% elsewhere; **`REQ_TREE_GROWTH` 27 of 407 (6.6%) vs 0.5% — 27 of the corpus's 28 tree-growth requests**; `REQ_MOTIVATION_CONTENT` 11 of 407 (2.7%) vs 1.4%.
- **Support by review.** `SUPPORT_QUESTION` 14 of 407 (3.4%) vs 0.5%.
- **The paywall belief.** `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` 6 of 407 (1.5%) — every such review in the corpus.
- **Less "free" praise and fewer outcomes.** `PRAISE_FREE` 11 of 407 (2.7%) vs 9.2%; `OUT_HELPS_MOTIVATES` 43 of 407 (10.6%) vs 17.9%; `U:praise_specific` 41.3% vs 56.5%.
- **The crash.** `BUG_CRASH` 45 of 407 (11.1%) — 44 in the crash week.
- **Public rating 4.92★ over 8,179 ratings** (55.2% of all public ratings in these storefronts); corpus gap +0.58★.

Signal labels use the US denominator (56); one review is 1.8%. **41 of the 56 are written in Russian**, 14 in English and 1 in Spanish. US reviews are recent (2023: 3, 2024: 10, 2025: 13, 2026: 30).

**What is distinctive about the US storefront (with counts):**

- **The crash hit harder.** `BUG_CRASH` 15 of 56 (26.8%) vs 9.0% elsewhere; 15 of the 56 US reviews were posted in the crash week.
- **Fewer widget and tree requests.** `REQ_WIDGET` 4 of 56 (7.1%) vs 14.7%; `REQ_TREE_GROWTH` 1 of 56.
- **Best-in-category and free.** `PRAISE_BEST` 4 of 56 (7.1%) vs 2.9%; `PRAISE_FREE` 4 of 56 (7.1%).
- **More votes.** `META_HAS_VOTES` 10 of 56 (17.9%) vs 8.1%, mostly on crash reports.
- **English-language US reviews** are praise and outcomes — `13349297730` "This helped me quit vaping", `12157232469` "not forcing ads or payments like every other app" — plus one price comparison with benefit-tracking competitors (`14510416958`).
- **Public rating 4.90★ over 1,071 ratings**; corpus gap +0.70★.

## 6.5 High-spend markets and high-review-volume markets (both defined)

**High-spend group — defined, not measured.** No spend or download data exists. For comparability with the other reports in this series the same list is used: **us, gb, au, ca, de, ch, fr, it, es, nl, be, at, no, fi, sg, ae**. Eleven are present (ch, nl, be, at, sg are not). **The US is 56 of the group's 83 reviews**, and most of those are Russian-language.

**High-review-volume group** — storefronts with ≥10 reviews: **ru, us, ua, kz, by**. A review-volume proxy, not a download ranking. A third, descriptive group — **Russian-speaking storefronts** (ru, ua, kz, by, am, tj, uz) — is shown because language, not storefront, organises this corpus.

**Reading:**

- **The high-spend group is the crash group.** `BUG_CRASH` 16 of 83 (19.3%) vs 9.2% outside it, driven by the US; mean 4.325★.
- **Widget and tree requests are a high-volume, Russian-speaking behaviour** — `REQ_WIDGET` 15.0% in the high-volume group vs 6.7% in the small storefronts; tree growth 28 of 28 in high-volume storefronts.
- **Small storefronts are warmer and more about monetisation**: mean 4.600★, 7 of 75 (9.3%) with monetisation friction, 2 crash reports.

## 6.7 Corpus versus public ratings

**Corpus 4.397★ vs 4.913★ across 14,806 public ratings (+0.515★).** Before the crash week the corpus averages 4.647★. Gaps: RU +0.58★, US +0.70★, UA +0.21★, KZ +0.45★, BY +0.01★. **Text reviews and public ratings agree that the app is liked; the difference is the crash week**, whose one-star reviews barely move a cumulative public mean.

# PART 7 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Method

Reviews have no `version` field and release notes are one line each (§2.2). Eras are defined from what the corpus and the release dates show:

- **E1 — Launch, "no ads" praise** (2023-01-31 → 2024-06-11): ends the day before the first ad complaint.
- **E2 — Ad complaints begin** (2024-06-12 → 2025-06-30): starts with the first `ADS_TOO_MANY` (`11374484206`).
- **E3 — No releases; requests pile up** (2025-07-01 → 2026-07-15): a **calendar** split of the 19-month release gap, ending the day 1.2.0 shipped.
- **E4 — 1.2.0 crash wave** (2026-07-16 → 2026-07-22): from the first to the last after-update crash report.
- **E5 — After 1.2.1** (2026-07-23 → 2026-09-06).

| Era | Label | Window | Days | n | Reviews / 30 days | Mean ★ | 5/4/3/2/1★ | 1–2★ share | Non-Russian | Under 60 chars | With votes |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| **E1** | Launch, 'no ads' praise | 2023-01-31 → 2024-06-11 | 498 | 88 | 5.30 | 4.62 | 68/13/4/0/3 | 3.4% | 15 (17.0%) | 42 (47.7%) | 16 (18.2%) |
| **E2** | Ad complaints begin | 2024-06-12 → 2025-06-30 | 384 | 177 | 13.83 | 4.64 | 137/28/6/1/5 | 3.4% | 29 (16.4%) | 85 (48.0%) | 12 (6.8%) |
| **E3** | No releases; requests pile up | 2025-07-01 → 2026-07-15 | 380 | 250 | 19.74 | 4.66 | 200/34/4/4/8 | 4.8% | 37 (14.8%) | 115 (46.0%) | 12 (4.8%) |
| **E4** | 1.2.0 crash wave | 2026-07-16 → 2026-07-22 | 7 | 69 | 295.71 | 2.41 | 9/9/11/12/28 | 58.0% | 5 (7.2%) | 27 (39.1%) | 15 (21.7%) |
| **E5** | After 1.2.1 | 2026-07-23 → 2026-09-06 | 46 | 30 | 19.57 | 4.70 | 25/3/1/0/1 | 3.3% | 10 (33.3%) | 12 (40.0%) | 0 (0.0%) |

## 7.3 The crash week

| Day | Reviews | Crash reports |
|---|---:|---:|
| 2026-07-16 | 2 | 2 |
| 2026-07-17 | 12 | 12 |
| 2026-07-18 | 13 | 12 |
| 2026-07-19 | 11 | 10 |
| 2026-07-20 | 14 | 13 |
| 2026-07-21 | 14 | 14 |
| 2026-07-22 | 3 | 1 |

- **69 reviews in seven days**, a rate of 295.71 per 30 days against 19.74 in E3; 64 crash reports; mean **2.41★**; 58.0% one- or two-star. Storefronts of the crash reports: RU 44, US 15, KZ 3, CA 1, TJ 1.
- **Release context** [external]: 1.2.0 on 2026-07-15 ("The app has been translated into more languages."), 1.2.1 on 2026-07-21 ("Improved application performance"), 1.2.2 on 2026-07-30 ("Improved application stability"). **Timing, not proven cause**; but 21 reviewers say the crash started after the update, 14 say it happens when the ad or banner loads, and 3 say it stops without internet.
- **Ads appear to be new in that build.** `14314195428` (#522) — "после добавления рекламы в приложение (ничего против этого не имею) приложение стало вылетать сразу же после запуска рекламы" *[after ads were added — I don't mind that — the app crashes as soon as the ad starts]*.
- **Recovery.** `14334098154` (#583, 2026-07-22) reports the fix; `14330378148` (#578) had promised "Если починят, исправлю на 5" *[if they fix it I'll change to 5]*. After 2026-07-22, 30 reviews contain no crash report; with that few reviews, **the absence is moderate evidence of a fix**, consistent with the 1.2.1 note.

## 7.4 What rose and what fell

- **The widget request never went away.** First on 2023-02-06; 17 of 88 E1 reviews (19.3%); still present in August 2026 (`14469862599`). Nineteen months passed without a release (2024-12-09 → 2026-07-15), and 1.2.0 did not add one.
- **Ads turned from praise to complaint.** `PRAISE_NO_FEW_ADS` runs from 2023-01-31; the first ad complaint is 2024-06-12 and the last 2026-06-20; gambling ads were reported on 2025-05-09 and 2025-09-23.
- **Outcomes and recovery rose.** `OUT_QUIT_ABSTAINING` 27 of 250 (10.8%) in E3 vs 4 of 88 (4.5%) in E1; `U:safety_sensitive` 15 of 250 (6.0%) in E3.
- **Maintenance doubts appeared in the release gap**: `NEGLECT_PERCEIVED` first on 2025-07-08.
- **Volume grew**: 5.30 reviews per 30 days in E1, 13.83 in E2, 19.74 in E3.

## 7.5 Early signals already present before E4

- A launch crash in September 2024 (`11735356386`, "сразу вылетает" *[crashes at once]*).
- Counter errors tied to the phone clock and attempts (`11644642764`, 2024-08-23; `11876692458`, 2024-10-26).
- Two crash-adjacent data fears: a habit that deleted itself (`11194993574`, 2024-04-24) and a reset after a week (`12207978135`, 2025-01-20).

---

# PART 8 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Every recommendation is tied to findings by section number and labelled **Immediate fix**, **Experiment** or **Research question**. For a team building in this category, the same items read as **what to copy and what to avoid**.

## 8.1 Immediate fix — ads must never be able to take the counter down (§0.2, §3.5, §7.3)

**Evidence:** `BUG_CRASH` = 65 (10.59%), 64 in one week after 1.2.0; `BUG_CRASH_AD_LOAD` = 14 (2.28%); `CRASH_OFFLINE_WORKAROUND` = 3 (0.49%); 40 of the 62 one- and two-star reviews; a reviewer who could not log a relapse (`14331606843`) and one who feared losing years of notes (`14313036902`).

**Recommendation:** initialise the ad SDK after the counter renders, catch and fail open on any ad load error, and test release builds with the network on and ads live on current iOS. Stage rollouts and watch crash rates by build for the first 48 hours. Since the same crash hit the developer's other app (`14317387419`), share the fix across the portfolio.

**Why first:** for this audience a crash is not an inconvenience — the counter *is* the commitment device, and "if a relapse happens I can't log it" is the product failing at its one job.

## 8.2 Immediate fix — block gambling, alcohol and tobacco ad categories (§0.6, §3.11)

**Evidence:** `ADS_HARMFUL_CONTENT` = 2 (0.33%) — betting, casinos and gambling ads inside the app (`12635810456`, `13174041563`); `SCAM_LANGUAGE` in one of them; `U:safety_sensitive` = 23 (3.75%).

**Recommendation:** enforce category exclusions (gambling, alcohol, tobacco and vaping, dating) at the ad network and verify them per storefront. Two reports clear no frequency threshold; the harm case does.

## 8.3 Experiment — a home-screen and lock-screen widget (§0.3, §3.8)

**Evidence:** `REQ_WIDGET` = 86 (14.01%), in every era since February 2023; 30 of 87 four-star reviews; the corpus's second- and third-most-voted reviews (33 and 32 votes); churn over its absence (`14103004927`); one purchase intent conditional on it (`10456809560`).

**Experiment:** ship a free counter widget (habit name, days, circle) and measure retention and daily opens; test premium widget styles inside the ad-free subscription as a separate arm. The widget is the product's mechanism — keeping the number visible — so gate styling, not the number.

## 8.4 Experiment — let progress be seen and celebrated (§0.4, §3.8)

**Evidence:** `REQ_TREE_GROWTH` = 28 (4.56%) with the most-voted review in the corpus (109 votes); `REQ_MOTIVATION_CONTENT` = 14 (2.28%); `REQ_NOTIFICATIONS` = 11 (1.79%); a reviewer who forgot the app in three days (`13305706545`); and a counter-signal — `13322337199` finds plain numbers calmer than praise.

**Experiment:** a tree that grows with the current streak (and does not wither on relapse — relapses are a note, not a punishment); optional milestone notifications off by default. Measure 7- and 30-day retention against the current static tree.

## 8.5 Immediate fix — make goals editable and explain them (§3.7)

**Evidence:** `U:goal_setting` = 25 (4.07%); new users who cannot find where to change "goal: 1" (`12689955719`, `12867989318`); a 7→30 jump and a 100-year goal (`10814511549`, `13804456581`, `14456135031`); a report that custom goals were removed (`13034811450`).

**Recommendation:** show on first run that goals advance automatically, allow a custom next milestone, and use a gentler ladder (1, 3, 7, 14, 30, 60, 90 days, then yearly).

## 8.6 Experiment — lifetime ad removal and a clearer free promise (§0.7, §5.5)

**Evidence:** `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` = 2 (0.33%); `SUBSCRIPTION_OBJECTION` = 2 (0.33%); `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` = 6 (0.98%), all Russian; `PAID_EXPLICIT` = 2 (0.33%), both for ad removal.

**Experiment:** add a one-time "remove ads forever" purchase beside the monthly and yearly plans, and state on the purchase screen that every feature stays free. Measure purchase rate and one-star "paid app" reviews in RU.

## 8.7 Immediate fix — design for recovery from self-harm and drugs (§0.5, §3.11)

**Evidence:** `USE_SELF_HARM` = 13 (2.12%); `USE_DRUGS` = 8 (1.30%); `USE_TREATMENT_CONTEXT` = 3 (0.49%); users asking for supportive relapse copy (`10995642477`, `14118955087`); negative wording noticed (`11848596914`).

**Recommendation:** when a habit is self-harm or a drug, show crisis and support resources in the articles and relapse flow for each supported language; use non-judgemental relapse copy; and keep ads out of that flow.

## 8.8 Research questions

- **What in 1.2.0 caused the crash** — a new ad banner, the localisation update, or both — and why did offline use avoid it?
- **Which ad categories are served** in Russian-speaking storefronts, and how often do gambling ads appear?
- **Do users want backup or transfer** (`REQ_BACKUP_SYNC` = 4, 0.65%), and how does that square with a "works offline" promise and a tracking privacy label for sensitive habits?
- **Is the perceived paywall real** — an onboarding offer, a trial screen, or a different app?

## 8.9 What this corpus says to copy — and not to copy

| Copy | Why |
|---|---|
| A minimal counter that makes a streak feel costly to break | `U:praise_any` = 476 (77.52%); "breaking big numbers hurts more than the urge to smoke" (§0.1) |
| Unlimited habits and a relapse note, free | `U:tracking_core_praise` = 72 (11.73%); switchers name it (§3.2) |
| A non-triggering, quiet interface | `PRAISE_DESIGN` = 39 (6.35%); `12476493945` (§0.1) |

| Don't | Why |
|---|---|
| Let an ad load path crash the app | 64 crash reports in one week (§0.2) |
| Serve gambling ads in an addiction app | two reports, one calling it a scam (§0.6) |
| Leave the most-requested feature unbuilt for years | widget requests in every era since 2023 (§7.4) |
| Ship a fixed goal ladder users cannot see or change | `U:goal_setting` = 25 (4.07%) (§3.7) |

---

# PART 9 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 9.A Counting rules

1. **Denominator** is 614 (every review in `reviews.jsonl`) unless the sentence names a narrower one: a star band, an era, a storefront (RU 407, US 56), the rest of the corpus, a market group, a language, or a monetisation segment. Segment rates are written "n of N".
2. **Codes are non-exclusive. Unions count each review once.**
3. **Mean ★** for a code is the mean rating of the reviews carrying it.
4. **Signal band** is applied to the share of the stated denominator (§1.5); Wilson 95% intervals throughout.
5. **Mechanical codes** — `META_LOW_INFO` (HTML-unescaped, stripped body under 60 characters), `META_HAS_VOTES` (`vote_count` > 0), `META_NON_RUSSIAN` (hand-assigned language not Russian and not emoji-only) — are assigned by the build, never by hand.
6. **`PAID_EXPLICIT`** requires the reviewer to say they paid. Intent is `WOULD_PAY_INTENT`; a belief that the app is paid is `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED`.
7. **`BUG_CRASH`** requires the app to crash, close itself or throw the user out. **`BUG_CRASH_AD_LOAD`** requires the reviewer to name an ad, ad screen or banner; a "white strip" (`14331731205`) is not counted.
8. **`OUT_QUIT_ABSTAINING`** requires a stated stop or abstinence; "помогает" *[helps]* alone is `OUT_HELPS_MOTIVATES`.
9. **`USE_*` codes** record the habit the reviewer names for themselves; habits named only as examples of what the app is for are not coded.
10. **`PRAISE_GENERIC`** is assigned when a review approves but names no quality or outcome; it never co-occurs with specific praise, an outcome or `PRAISE_BEST` (check C1).
11. **`META_RATING_TEXT_MISMATCH`** is four or five stars with no praise code and at least one negative code, or one or two stars with praise and no negative code (checks C5–C6).
12. **Eras** are E1 2023-01-31 → 2024-06-11, E2 2024-06-12 → 2025-06-30, E3 2025-07-01 → 2026-07-15, E4 2026-07-16 → 2026-07-22, E5 2026-07-23 → 2026-09-06.
13. **Language** is hand-assigned and checked against Cyrillic, Ukrainian-letter, Latin, kana/Han, Arabic and Vietnamese script tests and an English function-word test (check L1).
14. **No deduplication** was performed; none was needed (§1.3).

## 9.B Taxonomy — all 79 codes

| Code | Dir | Family | n | Definition |
|---|:-:|---|---:|---|
| `PRAISE_ARTICLES` | + | Praise | 3 | Values the articles / useful information section. |
| `PRAISE_BEST` | + | Praise | 20 | Best / favourite / top app of its kind, or no equal found. |
| `PRAISE_COUNTER_PROGRESS` | + | Praise | 48 | Values seeing the counter (days to seconds), progress, records, goals, or 'not wanting to reset' the number. |
| `PRAISE_DESIGN` | + | Praise | 39 | Design, look, interface, colours, tree icon or dark theme called beautiful, stylish, aesthetic or non-triggering. |
| `PRAISE_FIX_CONFIRMED` | + | Praise | 1 | Confirms the developer fixed a problem. |
| `PRAISE_FREE` | + | Praise | 30 | Values that it is free / no subscription required / all functions without payment. |
| `PRAISE_GENERIC` | + | Praise | 184 | Approves but names no product quality ('good', 'love it', 'thanks', emoji). |
| `PRAISE_LOCALISATION` | + | Praise | 3 | Values the app being in the reviewer's language or offering many languages. |
| `PRAISE_MULTI_HABITS` | + | Praise | 17 | Values tracking several or unlimited habits at once, or any kind of addiction. |
| `PRAISE_NOTES_LOG` | + | Praise | 21 | Values notes/comments, the relapse log with reason or date, or writing about the journey. |
| `PRAISE_NO_FEW_ADS` | + | Praise | 19 | Says there are no ads, few ads, or that ads are acceptable / not intrusive. |
| `PRAISE_SIMPLE_EASY` | + | Praise | 132 | Simple, minimal, 'nothing extra', convenient, easy, intuitive or understandable to use. |
| `OUT_HELPS_MOTIVATES` | + | Outcome | 80 | Says the app helps, motivates, disciplines or keeps them from relapsing, without a concrete quit claim required. |
| `OUT_NOT_HELPED_RELAPSE` | ~ | Outcome | 11 | Reports a relapse, no success yet, or that the app cannot help by itself. |
| `OUT_QUIT_ABSTAINING` | + | Outcome | 42 | Says they quit, stopped, or are currently abstaining (often with a duration). |
| `ACQ_SOCIAL` | o | Use | 2 | Found the app through a video / social media, or friends also use it. |
| `DEV_OTHER_APPS` | o | Use | 5 | Mentions the developer's other apps. |
| `SEG_LONG_TENURE` | o | Use | 8 | States use for about six months or more ('3 years', '1000 days', 'a long time'). |
| `SEG_TRIED_OTHERS` | o | Use | 17 | Compares with, or says they tried, other apps of this kind. |
| `USE_ALCOHOL` | o | Use | 10 | Alcohol / drinking / sobriety. |
| `USE_DRUGS` | o | Use | 8 | Illicit or misused drugs (cocaine, 'krokodil', injected drugs, weed, Lyrica, antidepressants, 'торчать'). |
| `USE_FOOD_WEIGHT` | o | Use | 8 | Sugar, sweets, diet, weight loss, or food restriction/starvation. |
| `USE_OTHER_HABIT` | o | Use | 12 | Other habit: phone addiction, skin picking, nail or lip biting, swearing, insomnia, caffeine, 'sins', a habit causing stuttering. |
| `USE_SELF_HARM` | o | Use | 13 | Self-harm / cutting ('резаться', 'селфхарм', 'слфх'). |
| `USE_SEXUAL` | o | Use | 3 | Masturbation, porn or a fetish. |
| `USE_SMOKING_VAPING` | o | Use | 17 | Smoking, vaping or nicotine. |
| `USE_TREATMENT_CONTEXT` | o | Use | 3 | Names clinical context: psychiatrist, rehab, antidepressants. |
| `PAID_EXPLICIT` | o | Paid users | 2 | Says they paid / bought the ad-free subscription. |
| `PAID_FOR_AD_REMOVAL` | o | Paid users | 2 | Names removing ads as the reason they paid. |
| `PAID_SATISFIED` | + | Paid users | 2 | A paying reviewer is satisfied. |
| `TRIAL_USER` | o | Paid users | 1 | Says they started a free trial. |
| `WOULD_PAY_INTENT` | ~ | Paid users | 3 | Would or intends to pay (possibly on a condition such as a widget or lifetime option). |
| `BILL_REFUND_REQUEST` | o | Billing | 1 | Asks for a refund. |
| `BILL_UNEXPECTED_CHARGE` | - | Billing | 2 | Charged, or charge attempted, when the reviewer expected free / had cancelled. |
| `ADS_HARMFUL_CONTENT` | - | Monetisation | 2 | Ads promote gambling / casinos (an addiction) inside an addiction app. |
| `ADS_TOO_MANY` | - | Monetisation | 18 | Ads named as a minus: too many, long, unskippable, every action, or unwelcome. |
| `FUTURE_MONETISATION_WORRY` | o | Monetisation | 3 | Hopes ads or fees will not be added later. |
| `PAYWALL_PERCEIVED` | - | Monetisation | 6 | Says the app is paid / fully paid / 'only for a month', or asks whether a feature is paid-only. |
| `PRICE_FAIR` | + | Monetisation | 1 | Calls the price reasonable. |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_OBJECTION` | - | Monetisation | 2 | Refuses or dislikes paying a subscription for this kind of app. |
| `WANT_ONE_TIME_PURCHASE` | ~ | Monetisation | 2 | Wants a lifetime / one-time purchase (e.g. to remove ads for life). |
| `BUG_AFTER_UPDATE` | - | Defects | 21 | Says a defect started after the latest update. |
| `BUG_COUNTER_ATTEMPTS` | - | Defects | 5 | Counter or attempts wrong: reset, wrong days, follows phone date, attempt 2 not set, deleted record still counts. |
| `BUG_CRASH` | - | Defects | 65 | App crashes, closes itself or throws the user out (on launch, after seconds, or on an action). |
| `BUG_CRASH_AD_LOAD` | - | Defects | 14 | Ties the crash explicitly to an ad / ad banner / ad screen loading. |
| `CRASH_OFFLINE_WORKAROUND` | o | Defects | 3 | Says the app works with airplane mode / internet off, or crashes only with internet on. |
| `DATA_LOSS` | - | Defects | 3 | A habit, its history, statistics or notes disappeared or are feared lost. |
| `FEATURE_THIN` | - | Defects | 12 | Too primitive, boring, 'just a timer', pointless, or wants more functions. |
| `NEGLECT_PERCEIVED` | - | Defects | 3 | App feels unmaintained / not updated for years. |
| `REDESIGN_DISLIKE` | - | Defects | 1 | An update made the design worse. |
| `UX_CONFUSING` | - | Defects | 5 | Confusing or inconvenient to use (outside goal setting): unclear at first, cannot delete/rename, layout order. |
| `GOAL_HOWTO_QUESTION` | o | Goals | 13 | Asks or does not understand how to set / change the goal, its duration, the timer or the start date. |
| `GOAL_STEPS_RIGID` | ~ | Goals | 15 | Wants to set their own goal; says goal steps are fixed, too large (7→30), unrealistic (100 years) or stuck at 1 day. |
| `REQ_BACKUP_SYNC` | ~ | Requests | 4 | Asks for an account, backup or transfer to a new device. |
| `REQ_BENEFITS_STATS` | ~ | Requests | 3 | Asks for health benefits, cigarettes not smoked, money or time saved. |
| `REQ_CALENDAR` | ~ | Requests | 4 | Asks for a calendar view of relapses / progress. |

## 9.C Validation, and what it caught

`90-validate.py` runs **101 checks, all passing** (output in `temp/90-validate-out.txt`; log in `temp/90-validation-log.md`): 10 structural, 4 mechanical, 1 language, 57 forward text-to-code ties, 18 reverse vocabulary tests with every hit printed and read, and 11 consistency rules.

**The first run failed 18 of 101 checks. Two hand-file lines were real classification errors, fixed:**

| Check | Review | Error | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | `13373717171` (#343) | generic praise co-coded with an abstinence outcome | `PRAISE_GENERIC` removed |
| T | `14434087198` (#598) | a hope to lose weight coded as early use | `META_EARLY_USE` removed |

**The other failures were defects in the checks**, each fixed: "давно" *[long ago]* matching inside "недавно" *[recently]*; "цел" matching "в целом" *[overall]*; "краш" inside "окрашивать" *[to colour]*; "пуш" inside "Пушка" *[slang: awesome]*; "часы" meaning hours, not a watch; missing vocabulary ("рецедив", "скачивания", Polish "funkcji"); and expected-code lists that lacked legitimate codes.

**Documented exemptions:** L1 — `14326307046` (English with its own Russian translation); five Ukrainian reviews without і/ї/є identified by vocabulary (`11170243898`, `11198839959`, `13173161133`, `13756895911`, `14234441517`); two reviews with letters but no language (`10741727426`, `12767381255`). T — `10423669605` shows multi-habit use without naming it. R11 — `11963488890` refers to a paper calendar.

**Known residual error risk.** "Помогает" *[helps]* alone is coded as an outcome, which lifts `U:outcome_any` and lowers generic praise. Drug mentions may be jokes (`10955988478`, a "крокодил" *[krokodil]* claim, and `13373717171`, antidepressants) or slang (`14155060016` "торчать" *[to be high]*). `SEG_TRIED_OTHERS` includes comparative wording that may not mean installing other apps. Checks enforce consistency, not correctness.

**Dropping the crash week** raises the mean to 4.650★ and removes the crash finding (10.6% → 0.2%); every other headline holds — the widget rises to 15.4%. **Dropping Russia** lowers the widget to 8.7% and tree growth to 0.5%: those are Russian-storefront findings. **Dropping the 281 short reviews** raises specific praise (46.4% → 53.5%) and tree growth (4.6% → 7.5%). **Dropping the mismatches** changes nothing material.

## 9.H Burst, solicitation and authenticity checks

- **No duplicate IDs, no exact title+body duplicates, no repeat authors, no edited reviews** (§1.3).
- **The only bursts are the crash week.** The busiest days are 2026-07-20 and 2026-07-21 (14 reviews each), 2026-07-18 (13) and 2026-07-17 (12), all crash reports; outside that week no day has more than four reviews (2025-11-27 and 2026-09-04, four each, all five-star). **An event response, not a positive burst.**
- **No solicitation language.** No review mentions a reward, survey, referral code or a request to rate.
- **Repeated short titles** ("Отличное приложение" 14, "Супер" 11, "Хорошее приложение" 8) are common phrasing with different bodies and authors.
- **Reviews echo each other on requests.** `META_CITES_OTHER_REVIEWS` = 8 (1.30%), mostly repeating tree growth or the widget; one claims reviews are bought (`10856120064`). The most-voted review (109 votes) is a tree-growth request, which likely seeded later ones.
- **Helpful votes** — 55 reviews, 277 votes; mean votes per review by band 0.51 / 0.30 / 0.27 / 0.35 / 0.36 (5★ → 1★), inflated at five stars by the 109-vote request.
- **Author names** include profanity and jokes in several Russian-storefront reviews; texts were classified on content only.

**Reading.** Nothing here looks like coordinated positive reviewing. The corpus's weakness is short text, not manipulation.

## 9.I Developer and support responses

**On the listing** [external]: no developer responses were found in the captured US or RU pages.

**In reviews:** no reviewer reports contacting support or receiving a reply. 15 reviews ask the developer a question directly (`SUPPORT_QUESTION`), and one confirms a fix without describing contact (`14334098154`).
